Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in semiconductors theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 617 and the invariant I(A,B) = 333 mod 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_617(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 617. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^333(S, F) is trivial. QED.
